# Tamago content review

Sentences live: 1729 (1409 matched to Tatoeba, 320 Tamago originals), 0 off, 1409 awaiting a ruling. Words with Wiktionary data: 465 of 465. French-to-English answers: 447 live, 0 off, 0 flagged. English-to-French answers generated from Tatoeba: 34.

Unit 1 is approved and live.

Generated by `npm run content`. Do not edit by hand: record decisions in content/review-decisions.json.

**What "checked" means here.** Tatoeba has no checked field. A sentence passes when its French is in Tatoeba exactly (ignoring curly apostrophes and spacing), a directly linked English translation matches the draft English or an accepted answer (I'm = I am), and the sentence is owned by a self-declared native French speaker or carries Tatoeba's OK tag. Dan can confirm a match that lacks one of these.
